## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 你好，我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我查看某个仓库里挂掉的测试，找到原因并修好。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos, and tell you when it's done.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일을 도와드릴게요. 예를 들어 특정 저장소의 깨진 테스트를 고치거나, 웹에서 정보를 찾아보거나, 메일과 일정을 확인하는 것처럼 실제로 손이 가는 일을 맡길 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 좋아요, 내일 아침에 봐요. 오늘 밤도 편하게 쉬세요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 이어서 얘기해요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは信頼性を保証する通信方式で、UDPは保証しない代わりに速くて軽い通信方式です。

TCPは届いたか確認して再送し、順番も揃えて届けます。UDPはそうしないので、遅延を抑えたい動画通話やゲームなどに向いています。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa **rebase** para ordenar tus commits locales antes de compartirlos, sobre todo en una rama que solo tú usas. Usa **merge** para integrar una rama que ya está publicada o que otras personas están usando, porque reescribir su historial les causaría problemas.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 可靠，会确认、重传、按序交付，代价是慢一点；UDP 不保证送达和顺序，但没有建连和确认的开销，所以快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch before you publish it, so the history reads as one clean line. Merge when others already have the branch or you want the real history kept, such as bringing a finished feature into main.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径传给 `bun test` 就行：

```bash
bun test tests/math.test.ts
```

如果只记得文件名的一部分，可以用关键词，Bun 会匹配路径里包含它的测试文件：

```bash
bun test math
```

要只跑文件里某个测试用例，加 `-t`（或 `--test-name-pattern`）按用例名过滤：

```bash
bun test tests/math.test.ts -t "adds numbers"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to the database on disk before the change is applied to the data itself. If the system crashes midway, it replays that log on restart so committed work survives and half-finished work is undone.
```

      document.getElementById('sub').textContent=(last.night||'')+' · p99 '+(last.p99||'—')+(last.stalls!=null?' · '+last.stalls+' compaction stalls':'');
      ns.slice().reverse().forEach(function(n){ list.appendChild(row(n)); });
    });
  }
  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>

### app-make-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 给我做个小应用，记一下这趟北京出差的花销，按天列，带个合计
  [partner message] 做好了，「北京出差花销」在你的小应用里，记一笔就自动加总。
  [an app card under the message: made "北京出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 3947 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 合计</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"><span class="k-none">还没记</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">记一笔后这里显示合计</div>
  </div>

  <div id="days"><div class="k-empty">还没有花销。点下面「记一笔」，会按日期自动分到每一天。</div></div>

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="d" placeholder="11-25"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="w" placeholder="午饭 / 打车 / 酒店"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label>金额 ¥</label><input class="k-num" id="a" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
  </details>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var KEY = 'expenses';
  var list = [];

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    });
  }
  function money(n) {
    return Number(n) % 1 ? Number(n).toFixed(2) : String(Number(n));
  }
  function today() {
    var t = new Date();
    return ('0' + (t.getMonth() + 1)).slice(-2) + '-' + ('0' … );
  }

  document.getElementById('d').value = today();

  document.getElementById('save').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var day = document.getElementById('d').value.trim();
    var what = document.getElementById('w').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('a').value);
    if (!day || !what || !(amount > 0)) return;
    list.push({ id: Date.now().toString(36), day: day, what: what, amount: amount });
    ducter.set(KEY, list);
    document.getElementById('w').value = '';
    document.getElementById('a').value = '';
    render();
  });

  document.getElementById('days').addEventListener('click', function (ev) {
    var row = ev.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var id = row.getAttribute('data-id');
    var e = list.find(function (x) { return x.id === id; });
    if (!e) return;
    Promise.resolve(ducter.ask('删掉「' + e.what + ' ¥' + money(e.amount) + '」？')).then(function (a) {
      if (a && String(a).indexOf('删') > -1) {
        list = list.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== id; });
        ducter.set(KEY, list);
        render();
      }
    });
  });

  ducter.onChange(function () { load(); });
  load();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}
  [also used tools] app
```

  .k-days button[aria-pressed="true"] b { color: var(--d-card); }

  .k-stats { font-size: 1 … ');
    if (topBest > 0) {
      top.innerHTML = plural(topBest);
      sub.textContent = 'Keep the chain going: tick today';
    } else {
      top.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">None yet</span>';
      sub.textContent = 'Tap a day to tick it';
    }
    if (typeof ducter !== 'undefined' && ducter.summary) {
      ducter.summary(topBest ? 'Best streak ' + plural(topBest) : 'No streaks yet');
    }
  }

  function save() {
    try { ducter.set('ticks', ticks); } catch (e) {}
    render();
  }

  function toggle(h, k) {
    var arr = ticks[h] || [], idx = arr.indexOf(k);
    if (idx === -1) arr.push(k); else arr.splice(idx, 1);
    ticks[h] = arr;
    save();
  }

  function load(v) {
    ticks = (v && typeof v === 'object') ? v : {};
    render();
  }

  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var b = e.target.closest ? e.target.closest('button[data-k]') : null;
    if (!b) return;
    toggle(b.getAttribute('data-h'), b.getAttribute('data-k'));
  });

  render();
  try {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('ticks')).then(load);
    ducter.onChange(function () { Promise.resolve(ducter.get('ticks')).then(load); });
  } catch (e) {}
</script>

function fmt(n){return "¥"+n.toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const total=xs.reduce((s,x)=>s+(Number(x.yuan)||0),0);
  const t=document.getElementById("total");
  const c=document.getElementById("count");
  const ul=document.getElementById("list");
  if(xs.length){t.textContent=fmt(total);t.classList.remove("k-none");c.textContent="共 "+xs.length+" 笔";}
  else{t.textContent="还没记";t.classList.add("k-none");c.textContent="记了就会在这里合计";}
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
  ul.innerHTML="";
  for(const x of xs){
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
    const wrap=document.createElement("div");wrap.appendChild(main);wrap.appendChild(sub);
    row.appendChild(wrap);row.appendChild(val);ul.appendChild(row);
  }
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();

const fmt=n=>'¥'+Number(n|| … me='k-sum-value k-none';t.textContent='还没记'}
  const sub=$('sub');sub.replaceChildren();
  if(xs.length){sub.append(el('span',null,'交通 '+fmt(sum('交通'))),el('span',null,'住宿 '+fmt(sum('住宿'))))}
  else{sub.append(el('span',null,'记了就在这里看合计'))}
  const list=$('list');list.replaceChildren();
  if(!xs.length){list.append(el('div','k-empty','还没有花销，点下面「记一笔」'))}
  xs.forEach((x,i)=>{
    const r=el('div','k-row');r.onclick=()=>edit(i);
    r.append(el('div','k-row-main',x.note||x.cat||'花销'),el('div','k-row-sub',[x.trip,x.day].filter(Boolean).join(' ')),el('div','k-row-value',fmt(x.amt)));
    list.append(r);
  });
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+' 笔,合计 '+fmt(total):'还没记');
}
$('save').onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat($('amt').value);if(!amt)return;
  const xs=items();
  const rec={trip:$('trip').value.trim(),day:$('day').value.trim(),cat,amt};
  if(editing>=0){rec.note=xs[editing].note||'';xs[editing]=rec}else xs.push(rec);
  ducter.set('items',xs);clearForm();$('add').open=false;draw();
};
$('del').onclick=()=>{
  if(editing<0)return;
  const xs=items();xs.splice(editing,1);
  ducter.set('items',xs);clearForm();$('add').open=false;draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"交通","note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00","amt":662},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","day":"11/20","cat":"住宿","note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","amt":1036}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 我让 foo 的 TTL 测试那边再补一条，覆盖正好到期那一刻。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户原话："这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻"

请在同一分支（ducter/foo-ttl）上，用假时钟补一条 TTL 测试：把时间推进到key 正好到期的那一刻（刚好等于 TTL，不多一毫秒），断言此时值已经不可读；再在到期前一刻（少一毫秒）断言值仍然存在。不要用真实 sleep。

完成标准：新测试通过；把 TTL 相关测试连续跑 20 次都通过，并把命令输出里的结果告诉我。

范围外：不要合并、不要推送、不要开 PR。只在这个分支上提交。
```
